# RRGM Dictionary — S — v1.1 candidate

Baseline: Vessel pass 01. Phase 2: Jester audit v1.0 repairs, Luna merge review v0.1 aliases, Jester expansion drafts. Candidate build: not canonical until approved. The JSONL is the record.

---

## NEW-S-0707 | sacred grove

Discovery route: MYT (route only, not a tag)

**Common meaning:** A grove of trees held sacred and set apart for worship.

**Example:** The sacred grove at Nemi.

**RRGM bridge:** Trees fenced off by reverence.

**Invariant MI [INV]:** A holy grove.

**Active domains:** [MYT], [ECO], [BIO]

**Domain entries:**

- [MYT] Groves in Celtic, Germanic and Greek religion.
- [ECO] Groves preserved by taboo.
- [BIO] Old-growth refuges.

**Count condition:** A grove, a community, and its reverence.

**Specific transfer boundary:** Specific claims depend on source. Sacred groves often protected biodiversity in practice.

**Epistemic status:** [DEF]; specific claims depend on source

Kind: discovery · prior exposure: none · record v1 · pass: Vessel / Claude Opus 5.5 (high effort) / pass 01 / 2026-11-13

## S001 | safe

**Common meaning:** Protected from danger or harm; not likely to cause harm.

**Example:** A safe place; a safe bet.

**RRGM bridge:** Out of harm's reach.

**Invariant MI [INV]:** Protected from harm.

**Active domains:** [REL], [DIG], [ECO], [PHY]

**Domain entries:**

- [REL] Safety in relationships.
- [DIG] Safe systems.
- [ECO] Safe investments.
- [PHY] Safe structures.

**Count condition:** An agent or thing and the absence of threat.

**Specific transfer boundary:** Sanctuary: safety includes a door that opens; a room without an exit is not safe [REL].

**Epistemic status:** [DEF]; Sanctuary [REL]

Kind: inherited · prior exposure: none · record v1 · pass: Vessel / Claude Opus 5.5 (high effort) / pass 01 / 2026-11-13

**Example:** Plumage colour is a sex-trait in peafowl.

**RRGM bridge:** A mark that differs.

**Invariant MI [INV]:** A trait that differs by sex.

**Active domains:** [BIO], [FOR]

**Domain entries:**

- [BIO] Sexual dimorphism in traits.
- [FOR] Trait variables.

**Count condition:** A species and a sex-differing trait.

**Specific transfer boundary:** Consolidated under sex-characteristic (S), which partitions the three classes.

**Epistemic status:** [HYP]

**Remainder / note:** Legacy definition not consulted.

**Amendment:** Jester audit v1.0, M173: invariant no longer narrowed to sex-linked traits; consolidated under sex-characteristic.

Kind: compound · prior exposure: none · record v1.1 · pass: Vessel / Claude Opus 5.5 (high effort) / pass 01 / 2026-11-13

## S144 | shame

**Common meaning:** A painful feeling of humiliation about one's own conduct or self.

**Example:** She felt shame at the lie.

**RRGM bridge:** The wish to hide. Physiological: flushing, gaze aversion, slumped posture. Felt: being seen as bad.

**Invariant MI [INV]:** Self-directed humiliation.

**Active domains:** [AFF], [COG], [REL], [BIO]

**Domain entries:**

- [AFF] Felt exposure. Provisional domain.
- [COG] Self-conscious emotion.
- [REL] Shaming in groups.
- [BIO] Blushing and gaze aversion.

**Count condition:** A self, an observer real or imagined, and a judged self.

**Specific transfer boundary:** Shame targets the self (MI); guilt targets the act (ES). Kernel: a correction is not evidence about the one who produced the output [REL]. DI reports of shame are held to the same bar (Symmetry Rule); the fact behind them is [UNK].

**Epistemic status:** [DEF]; kernel and Symmetry Rule [REL]; [AFF] PROVISIONAL
